What makes the perfect city bike?
Thoughtful Design
Not all city bikes are created equal. We spend hours agonizing over the smallest details, and you'll be hard-pressed to find another brand putting as much thought into their bikes as us.
Premium Componentry
Technical details that a casual rider might never notice make a world of difference in the comfort, reliability, and maintenance cost of a bicycle.
Weather Resistance
We save you time and money by outfitting your bike properly from the beginning- including stainless steel parts, rust-resistant chains, and water-resistant grips and saddles.
Professional Assembly
Designed for city life—your Brooklyn city bike will be professionally assembled at a local shop for a smooth, reliable ride—at no cost to you.

A beach cruiser that's more than just a beach cruiser.
Our debut cruiser bike, the Brighton 7 is available in an inspiring array of colors, making it your ideal beach accessory. The carefully crafted frame geometry and Selle Royal wide comfort saddle combine to make this beach bike as comfortable as it is fun. 7 speeds and wide cruiser tires will take you from Broadway to the boardwalk with ease.
- External 7-speed drive train
- Ideal for cruises around the neighborhood
- Super relaxed & comfort focused geometry
- Mounting points for accessories
- NOTE: Fenders are not included or offered with this model
FRAME
Frame: Lightweight 6061-T6 Aluminum Frame with rear carrier & down tube braze-ons, pure comfort pedal-forward geometry
Fork: Brooklyn Bicycle Co. custom steel fork with fender & front carrier braze-ons
DRIVETRAIN
Front Hub: Low Flange Alloy 32H with Quick Release
Rear Hub: Low Flange Alloy 32H with Quick Release
Shifter: Shimano Revoshift 7-Speed Shifter
Derailluer: Shimano 7 Speed
Pedals: Brooklyn Bicycle Co. Resin Pedals with Non-Slip Rubber Insert
Bottom Bracket: Square-taper BSA English threaded-cartridge bottom bracket with sealed bearings
Crank: 3-piece integrated
Chain: KMC Heavy Duty with Rust Prohibitive Coating
WHEELS
Tires: 26x1.95" Cream Tires all models except Ivory & Black models have black tires (As per studio images)
Rims: Double-Walled Painted Alloy, 32H with CNC Machined Brake Surface
Spokes: 14G stainless steel
COMPONENTS
Saddle: Selle Royal Comfort Sprung Saddle
Seat Post: Alloy with Quick Release Clamp
Handlebar: Brooklyn Bicycle Co. custom upright swept back alloy handlebars & alloy stem
Headset: 1 1/8" Threaded Alloy, Semi-Integrated with weather-seal ring
Grips: Selle Royal Handstitched Grips
Front & Rear Brakes: Alloy linear-pull brakes with alloy levers
OTHER INFORMATION
Extras: Alloy center-mount kickstand, Internal Cable Routing, Stainless Anti-Rust Hardware
Weight: 33 lbs

Our industry leading Buy & Ride program was the first of its kind. Through years of experience, and a large network of partner shops across the country, we've perfected the process.

Four decades after I last rode a bike I decided to get back in the saddle, to help with the balance and strength issues associated with getting, ummmm, more mature. After lots of research, I chose the Franklin 3-speed. It not only came with all of the features I was looking for, but it's the most beautifully designed bike on the market. On my maiden voyage around the neighborhood, people yelled out "Nice bike!" I've never gotten so much attention!
The Franklin is a dream to ride; it's lightweight, the gears shift easily, and the handlebars are the perfect width and angle. I ordered the rear rack separately and installed it myself with ease.
My biggest concern was the logistics of having to pick up the bike at a local shop, but that process was flawless. The shop had the bike ready and waiting for me within a day of receiving it, and adjusted everything to fit me when I came to pick it up. I was out the door in minutes, and on the road.

I've had my Roebling for around six months now. It's been a good experience. The key use for me is for utility rides, but sometimes for athletic rides too. Everything has really checked out with it. Brakes, gears, tires, etc. The layout with the bike tends to support my efficient ride style, where I want the speed I can get from it, where I'm tucked into it, and am able to respond to any traffic problems. I did have one flat early on with it and patched it. For myself, while the tires are good, and are the same size compared to what I sized down to on my Marin hybrid (35C) I'm planning on sizing down to 32C with this for the speed and city streets. I did have an issue with the left rear brake arm locking on me, but when I removed the arm, it was obviously related to too much crud built up or left. Cleaned and lubed and it straightened out for me. The other three haven't done that yet, but now I know the take down for those. The 29 pound weight is reasonable and three lbs. under what the Marin weighs, and five over what my steel State road bike weighs.

Sporty, lightweight, and ready for anything.
One of our most versatile commuter bikes, the Roebling features a wide gearing range and puncture-resistant tires for all-day and all-terrain riding. But don’t let this sleek hybrid bike fool you: It’s just as ideal for commuting and running errands as it is for working up a sweat on city streets or bike paths. The ultra-lightweight, double-butted chromoly steel frame delivers a light and smooth ride everywhere life takes you. While similar in design to its sibling the Lorimer, the Roebling sets itself apart with a longer cockpit and sportier, more contoured saddle.
- 9-speed drive train
- Lightweight Chromoly frame
- Hybrid geometry for a quick & responsive ride
- Mounting points for accessories
FRAME
Frame: Lightweight Double-Butted 4130 CrMO Hybrid Frame with fender/rack mount eyelets
Fork: Brooklyn Bicycle Co. custom steel fork with fender & front carrier eyelets
DRIVETRAIN
Front Hub: Alloy 32H with Quick Release
Rear Hub: Alloy 32H with Quick Release
Shifter: Quick-fire Trigger Shift
Rear-Derailleur: Shimano 9-Speed
Cassette: Wide-Range Shimano 11:41
Crankset: 42t with Double Alloy Chain Cover
Pedals: Brooklyn Bicycle Co. Alloy City Pedals
Bottom Bracket: Square-taper BSA English threaded-cartridge bottom bracket with sealed bearings
Chain: KMC 9 Speed
WHEELS
Tires: Puncture-resistant 700cx35
Rims: Double-Walled Alloy, 32H with CNC Machined Brake Surface
Spokes: 14G stainless steel
COMPONENTS
Saddle: Selle Royal Ergo Touring Saddle
Seat Post: Alloy with integrated seat clamp and micro-adjust
Handlebar: Brooklyn Bicycle Co. Custom Urban Flat Bar
Headset: 1 1/8" SlimStack with Alloy Top Cap
Grips: Brooklyn Bicycle Co. Double Density Ergo Comfort Grips with End Plug
Front & Rear Brakes: Alloy linear-pull brakes with front power modulator and alloy brake levers
OTHER INFORMATION
Extras: Eyelets and rack mounts for easy install of bottle cage, carrier and fenders
Weight: 28-30 lbs

Great question, and one we are asked often. You may have noticed that the Franklin 3 and the Franklin 8 are the same price-- this isn't a mistake! The Franklin 3 features a 3-speed internally geared rear hub (hello, super low-maintenance and ultra-durability), while the Franklin 8 features a 1x8 drivetrain with an external derailleur (hello, wider gearing range). There's a trade-off between the two, but the better choice will truly depend upon your use case. URL: https://brooklynbicycleco.com/pages/thoughtful-design
================================================================================
Function First
You don't need to know anything about design to know you like how a bike looks. But we want you to be confident that we've got the brains to back up our good looks. How comfortable your bike is, the ease and grace with which it tackles the road, how long it lasts, and how easy it is to maintain, are all functions of your bike's design - the calculations that go into each angle, the placement of each part, the alignment of each element with each other. If a bicycle is visually appealing, that's a matter of aesthetics, not design. We prioritize both.
Many brands use factory standard frame geometry-often throwing their logo on whatever frames their factory has available at the time. We think you should have a frame that's designed more methodically, so that when you get your seat and handlebars just right, everything else will fall into place.
That's why our bikes are designed with input from industry expert Grant Petersen (Rivendell Bicycles), whose design ethos can be seen in most of our models. Grant is widely known in the cycling community for opposing complicated design and slavish prioritization of speed, light weight and racing-led fashion — over tradition, comfort, and durability. With his assistance, we've agonized over millimeters of frame geometry to ensure that you have a bike that'll assuredly be a pleasure to ride.
The heart of your bike is the frame. The bulk of our frame tubing is 4130 Chromoly steel, a stronger metal than Hi-Ten steel or aluminum. Its superior strength allows us to use thinner tubing without compromising quality. This results in a lighter, more durable bike. In addition, steel is a more flexible metal than aluminum, affording riders a more forgiving bicycle, and therefore a more comfortable ride.
Not all wheels are created equal. Ours offer hubs with sealed bearings, which not only afford a smooth ride, but also keep water from rusting the wheel from the inside out. Our rims are double-walled for twice the durability, and offer a machined brake surface for smoother stops. To protect our tires against flats and our rims against damage, nylon rim strips are standard on all our bikes, and all models are come stock with puncture resistant protection.
You'll be hard-pressed to find another brand putting as much thought into their wheels as we do. Technical details that a casual rider might never notice make a world of difference in the comfort, reliability, and cost of maintenance of a bicycle.
At Brooklyn Bike Co., we say: Enjoy your ride. Let us worry about the details!
We outfit our bikes with all aluminum and stainless steel parts--plus on some models, a rust inhibiting coating on the chain--to forestall rusting. Then we add grips and saddles of vegan leather, a water-resistant material. We finish things off with a special phosphate bath and a triple-coat of paint to keep your bike looking fresher longer.
Lots of brands offer one or two of these same features - but rarely all on one bicycle. A bike with little in the way of weather resistance means more trips to the shop - and more money out of your pocket.
We save you time and money by outfitting you properly from the beginning, so your bike will be something you can ultimately pass down to your children.

BEFORE I BUY
We are so excited to welcome you into the Brooklyn Bicycle Co. family. There are several factors to consider when choosing your new ride.

We also recommend taking our Bike Finder Quiz which will point you in the right direction based upon your considerations.
And finally, it's important to note that bike sizing is not universal across brands, nor across models. Each bike has a unique geometry which means different sizing. Please consult the sizing guide recommendations on each model page.
If you have any questions or hesitations, please contact us for personalized guidance and we'd be more than happy to assist!
Brooklyn Bicycle Co. bikes are available in-store throughout the US & Canada. Use our dealer locator to find a shop near you. Don’t worry if you don’t see a shop in your area; wherever you are, we’ll contact a local shop and arrange for your bike to be built at no extra charge to you. That's our Buy & Ride program.
You can still purchase a Brooklyn Bicycle! No matter where you are, we will contact a local shop and arrange to have your bike built at no extra charge to you. That’s our Buy & Ride program.
We would never ask our customers to assemble a bike by themselves - that’s why we devised our Buy & Ride program. Instead of arriving in pieces on your doorstep, your bike is shipped to a local shop where professionals properly assemble it, and make sure everything is perfectly adjusted for you, long before you hit the pavement.
If you have a local shop in mind when making your purchase, we would love to hear about them! Shoot us an email including the shop's name and address ASAP after making your purchase. We’re sure your local shop would appreciate their customers asking for them by name, and while we can’t guarantee shipment of your bike to that particular shop, we will absolutely look into the recommendation! (For example, if we have an established dealer in your area, we'd want to do right by them and give them your business.)
They absolutely do. Brooklyn Bicycle Co. frames are warrantied against manufacturing defects for the life of the original owner, while parts and hardware are guaranteed for one year after purchase. This warranty is non-transferrable and covers only manufacturing defects, not user-incurred damage, unforeseen accidents, and/or everyday wear and tear.
If you’ve already purchased one of our bikes, we recommend registering your warranty. This will expedite the processing of any claims you may make. If you have a warranty question or concern, please submit a support request.

We are so excited to hear that you are purchasing a bicycle as a gift. A bike is an awesome gift that isn't just an item to unwrap, but an experience to enjoy!
When purchasing a bicycle for someone, the first and most important thing to find out is their height and inseam to ensure proper fit of the bicycle. If you have questions regarding sizing, please give us a call at 800.631.0630 - we'd love to help!
Be sure to keep in mind color choice when purchasing a bicycle as a gift. Our bikes will last years to come, so we recommend you find out the recipient's color preferences. If you are unsure about which color to choose or are trying to keep the gift top-secret, we recommend going with a more neutral color like Ivory or Black.
Please take note of our Return and Exchange Policy before purchasing a bicycle as a gift. Returned and exchanged bicycles incur a $200.00 USD restocking fee. We understand that a restocking fee of $200.00 may seem excessive, however we pay to have the original bike shipped and assembled to your local bike shop, then disassembled and shipped back to us. We then pay to have the new bike shipped and assembled. All of this is included in the flat restocking fee.
Not sure where to start with gifting a bicycle? That's okay - we also offer gift cards! The gift card will be delivered electronically immediately to the recipient's e-mail address along with your personalized message. Our eGiftCards are available in denominations ranging from $50 - $1,000 USD. eGiftCards can be printed, although it is not necessary to do so for redemption. Please note gift cards are nonrefundable.
Sometimes FedEx doesn't love us as much as we love them. Sometimes there's a ding or a scratch. Sometimes we have excess stock- whether bikes or accessories. No matter the instance, our loss is your win.
NOTE: While factory deal bicycles may have slight cosmetic blemishes, rest assured, your bike will still be fully warrantied.

TRACKING & SHIPPING
We ask for about 14 business days, simply because there are factors out of our control which impact timing-- such as carrier transit times and assembly schedules at your local bike shop. While we ask for 14 business days, more often than not, you'll be riding sooner.
Please note that business days exclude holidays and weekends.
We do not typically share tracking information on bikes as it's not an accurate representation of when your bike will be ready for pickup. Instead, we ask for about 14 business days (excluding holidays and weekends) from the time of shipment to the time you'll be riding. Once your bike is ready for pickup, you will be notified with pickup details.
All accessories on your order will ship directly your shipping address. You will receive a tracking number for all accessory shipments. Please note we cannot accommodate shipment to the assembling bike shop, and we do not cover the cost of professional installation should you choose to get your accessories professionally assembled.
NOTE: All bags by North Street & San Util are shipped directly from them.
At this time, we only ship within the contiguous United States. If you're located elsewhere, we recommend entering your address on our Store Locator to check if there are any bicycle shops near you that carry our brand.
Please note we cannot accommodate shipment to freight forwarders.
We ship via FedEx Ground, UPS Ground, or USPS to the contiguous US. Accessory shipping speeds vary depending on warehouse availability, your shipping destination, and the accessory you've purchased.
We are unable to accomodate international shipping or shipping to freight forwarders.
BICYCLE PICKUP
You will receive an email and/or a phone call when your bike is ready for pickup. Sometimes our emails end up in junk or spam folders, so be sure to check these for an email from us!
Please bring a government issued I.D. to your local bike shop when you pick up your order. You may also be asked to show your email confirmation that your order is ready for pickup. If someone else is picking up your order on your behalf, please notify our team ahead of time.
We recommend you register your warranty. Please note your warranty is activated upon professional installation of your bike. Additionally, please note we do not capture serial numbers; we will only have your serial number on file if you register it with us. Your serial number can be found stamped into your frame's bottom bracket underneath where the pedal arms pass through.
The serial number of your bike is stamped under the bottom bracket of the bike. To find it, simply flip your bike over, and focus your attention on the part of the frame where the right and left pedals connect in the middle. You may need a flashlight to see the number, as it's not a sticker; the numbers are stamped directly into the metal. You can read more about how to locate your serial number via our journal post on the topic. If you have questions, feel free to give us a ring at 800.631.0630, and we'll walk you through it.
In an effort to decrease bicycle theft, there are several options available for bicycle registration. The greatest chance of recovering a stolen bicycle is by having a registered serial number. We strongly recommend registering the serial number of your bike with us.

ABOUT US
Our bikes are designed in Brooklyn—by our in-house team in collaboration with members of our Board of Advisors, a select group of industry experts—and manufactured in China and Taiwan.
Still not sure about our bikes? Check out our Factory page for a peek inside our manufacturing facilities.
You might not know this, but there are almost no large-scale bicycle manufacturing facilities in the entire US—let alone in Brooklyn specifically. We would love to manufacture our bikes here in the US, but it’s not actually possible to get bicycle parts made here in the States. The only significant bicycle manufacturing process that takes place in the States is frame welding; the other 90% of any bicycle is coming from overseas. The way things stand, we are able to provide the most value by manufacturing every element of our products in one place.
When it comes to quality, we are uncompromising, and after meeting with bicycle industry experts across the US and Europe, we came to the conclusion that the factories in China and Taiwan are the best the world has to offer. Close to 99% of new bicycles on the market are made and assembled there—and when you think about it—people doing this kind of work day in and day out, and the factories turning out hundreds of bicycles a day are, logically, the most well-equipped for the job. We chose to work with manufacturers whom we believe surpass all others when it comes to superb craftsmanship and attention to detail, and we’re proud of that.

Great question, and one we are asked often. You may have noticed that the Franklin 3 and the Franklin 8 are the same price-- this isn't a mistake! The Franklin 3 features a 3-speed internally geared rear hub (hello, super low-maintenance and ultra-durability), while the Franklin 8 features a 1x8 drivetrain with an external derailleur (hello, wider gearing range). There's a trade-off between the two, but the better choice will truly depend upon your use case. The Willow 7 is a premium super commuter featuring an aluminum frame, an internal 7-speed hub, and a hydraulic disc brakes. You can read more about the similarities and differences here.
Absolutely! Step-through models are well suited for carrying cargo because they're so easy to mount without having to throw your leg over a top tube. This makes mounting a rear child seat a breeze. We believe everyone has a right to quality products at affordable prices, which is why we work so hard to make sure our bikes and accessories are designed with the highest standards, and made with the best materials in the best facilities. Our warranty against manufacturing defects covers our frames for the lifetime of the original owner and covers our parts for a year after purchase. That’s how certain we are of the quality of our products--and how much we care about the people with whom we do business.
We’re based in Brooklyn, as our name suggests, and our bicycles are named after streets we traverse daily, so when you see us developing our brand, what you’re really seeing is us exploring our communities--Brooklyn of course, but also the myriad unexpected places that every new connection takes us. We take pride in who we are, and that means you can take pride in owning our products.
The respect we feel for our customers is apparent in every aspect of our business-- pricing, materials, service. We’ll even tell you if it seems like our bikes aren’t what you’re looking for-- because we genuinely want you to find the bike that’s right for you. This same philosophy carries over to our dealers, and to the folks who keep things moving in our office. We are easy to do business with, and believe in paying our employees what they’re worth, and in creating an environment where everyone feels comfortable contributing, because when one of us grows, we all grow.

I've had my Roebling for around six months now. It's been a good experience. The key use for me is for utility rides, but sometimes for athletic rides too. Everything has really checked out with it. Brakes, gears, tires, etc. The layout with the bike tends to support my efficient ride style, where I want the speed I can get from it, where I'm tucked into it, and am able to respond to any traffic problems. For myself, while the tires are good, and are the same size compared to what I sized down to on my Marin hybrid (35C) I'm planning on sizing down to 32C with this for the speed and city streets. The 29 pound weight is reasonable and three lbs. under what the Marin weighs, and five over what my steel State road bike weighs.

Where will my accessories ship?
Any accessory on your order will ship directly to you, which is why it's imperative to enter the correct shipping address at checkout. Please note we cannot accommodate shipment to the assembling bike shop or to a PO box.

Whether you are a hotel, corporate office, residential community, bike tour provider, or a bike shop with rental amenities, we offer a variety of options to get you going!
With 12 colors and 8 styles to choose from, we can find the perfect ride for you whether you're a beachfront hotel or a start-up in the city.
Branding and customization options ensure that bikes stay unique to your organization. Branded bicycle frames, numbered fenders, custom colors, and custom rear crates are just a few ways we can highlight your brand.
Technical details that a casual rider might never notice make a world of difference in the comfort, reliability, and maintenance cost of a bicycle.
Low order minimums so whether you need 5 bikes or 500, we've got you covered.
Hotel bike fleets allow guests to experience your city from a fresh and exciting new point of view, while increasing brand awareness amongst your community. Our Brighton 7 Cruiser is perfect for coastal riding while our Bedford and Franklin models are favorites for city exploration.
Whether you are looking to promote health initiatives at your corporate office, or want to relieve congestion in the company parking lot, bike fleets are a great way to delight employees and encourage sustainability.
Residential bike fleets are an exciting tool to create awareness about new developments and attract new residents. They are also an ideal way to promote a sense of community among neighbors and enable residents to see their neighborhood from a new perspective.

I've had my Roebling for around six months now. It's been a good experience. The key use for me is for utility rides, but sometimes for athletic rides too. Everything has really checked out with it. Brakes, gears, tires, etc. The layout with the bike tends to support my efficient ride style, where I want the speed I can get from it, where I'm tucked into it, and am able to respond to any traffic problems. I did have one flat early on with it and patched it. For myself, while the tires are good, and are the same size compared to what I sized down to on my Marin hybrid (35C) I'm planning on sizing down to 32C with this for the speed and city streets. I did have an issue with the left rear brake arm locking on me, but when I removed the arm, it was obviously related to too much crud built up or left. Cleaned and lubed and it straightened out for me. The other three haven't done that yet, but now I know the take down for those. The 29 pound weight is reasonable and three lbs. under what the Marin weighs, and five over what my steel State road bike weighs.

Comfortable, Reliable, and Stylish
Our essential city bike is a fan favorite for getting around town without working up a sweat. Stylish and utilitarian, the Franklin 3 enables riders of all stripes to simply hop on and go. The step-through frame and color-matched fenders ensure easy riding sans wardrobe restrictions, while the lightweight design makes for smooth travel from brunch, to the office, and everywhere in between. With a low-maintenance 3-speed internally geared hub and puncture-resistant tires allowing you to conquer varied terrain, this bike is as versatile as it is stylish.
- Internal 3-speed. Also available in 8-speed external
- Relaxed and upright geometry
- Mounting points for carriers
FRAME
Frame: Lightweight steel frame, rear carrier & down tube braze-ons
Fork: Brooklyn Bicycle Co. custom steel fork with fender & front carrier braze-ons
DRIVETRAIN
Front Hub: Sealed-Bearing Alloy 32H
Rear Hub: Shimano NEXUS 3-Speed internally geared
Shifter: Shimano NEXUS Revo 3-Speed Shifter
Pedals: Brooklyn Bicycle Co. Alloy City Pedals
Bottom Bracket: Square-taper BSA English threaded-cartridge bottom bracket with sealed bearings
Crank: 3-piece alloy with replaceable chain ring & integrated chain guard
Chain: KMC Zinc- and chrome-treated to resist corrosion and rusting
WHEELS
Tires: Puncture-resistant 650b with high visibility reflective stripe
Rims: Double-Walled Alloy, 32H with CNC Machined Brake Surface
Spokes: 14G stainless steel
COMPONENTS
Saddle: Vegan Leather Comfort Saddle
Seat Post: Alloy with integrated seat clamp and micro-adjust
Handlebar: Brooklyn Bicycle Co. Custom relaxed ride alloy handlebars & 180mm alloy stem
Headset: 1” Threaded Alloy, Semi-sealed with weather-seal ring
Grips: Vegan Leather Hand-Stitched Comfort Grips
Front & Rear Brakes: Alloy linear-pull brakes with alloy brake levers
OTHER INFORMATION
Extras: Heavy-duty alloy center-mount kickstand, full front & rear color-matched fenders, eyelets for easy install of bottle cage
Weight: 33-34 lbs
Every bicycle includes free shipping AND professional assembly through our Buy & Ride Program. Once your bike ships, you'll be riding within 14 business days.
Our number one priority is making your bicycle purchase as simple as possible- while also ensuring the bicycle you receive is up to our stringent quality requirements. That's why we ship every one of our bicycles to a hand-selected local bike shop for professional assembly. Once it's fully assembled and ready to ride, you simply head into the shop and roll out the door!
Our industry leading Buy & Ride program was the first of its kind. Through years of experience, and a large network of partner shops across the country, we've perfected the process.
There are several factors to consider when choosing your new bike, and we're here to help!
We highly recommend reading our Bike Buying Guide, along with watching the video. You'll want to consider your preferences on things like frame style and gearing range, and take into account how you'll be using your bike, and on what terrain.
We also recommend taking our Bike Finder Quiz which will point you in the right direction based upon your considerations.
And finally, it's important to note that bike sizing is not universal across brands, nor across models. Each bike has a unique geometry which means different sizing. Please consult the sizing guide recommendations.
If you have any questions or are between sizes, please contact us for personalized guidance and we'd happy to assist!
Brooklyn Bicycle Co. frames and forks are warrantied against manufacturing defects for life under the original owner, while parts and hardware are guaranteed for one year after purchase. This warranty is non-transferrable and covers manufacturing defects, not user-incurred damage, unforeseen accidents, and/or everyday wear and tear. You can read our full warranty here.

Great question, and one we are asked often. You may have noticed that the Franklin 3 and the Franklin 8 are the same price-- this isn't a mistake! The Franklin 3 features a 3-speed internally geared rear hub (hello, super low-maintenance and ultra-durability), while the Franklin 8 features a 1x8 drivetrain with an external derailleur (hello, wider gearing range). There's a trade-off between the two, but the better choice will truly depend upon your use case. The Willow 7 is a premium super commuter featuring an aluminum frame, an internal 7-speed hub, and a hydraulic disc brakes. You can read more about the similarities and differences here.
Absolutely! Step-through models are well suited for carrying cargo because they're so easy to mount without having to throw your leg over a top tube. This makes mounting a rear child seat a breeze. NOTE: We always recommend using the rear carrier from the child seat manufacturer as it will have been tested for load capacity.

What makes the perfect comfort bike?
Upright Riding Position
For easy and comfortable riding, with reduced pressure on your wrists and back
Cushy Sprung Saddle
For keeping your bottom comfortable, as far as the road will take you
All-Weather Components
For ensuring your comfort bike looks and rides amazing for as long as it's at your side

Crafted for effortless rides—your Brooklyn comfort bike will be professionally assembled at a local shop for a smooth, easy ride—at no cost to you.
Comfort Bikes
Expert Opinions on Our Comfort Bikes
Comfort Bike FAQs
What are comfort bikes?
A comfort bike is a relaxed, easy-to-ride bicycle designed for smooth, cushioned rides with an upright position, plush saddle, and shock-absorbing features.
What are comfort bikes good for?
Comfort bikes are perfect for leisurely rides, casual commuting, and paved paths, offering a smooth and comfortable cycling experience.
What are the differences between comfort bikes and cruiser bikes?
Comfort bikes are built for smooth, easy riding with an upright position and multi-gear options, while cruiser bikes typically focus on style and simplicity, with a laid-back design.
What makes Brooklyn cruiser bikes better?
It boils down to three things:
- We believe professional assembly is not a convenience, it's a necessity, that's why we include professional assembly on every bike purchase- free of charge to you.
- Our reviews speak for themselves. We stand behind our products, use premium components, and offer a lifetime warranty on frames and forks.
- Brooklyn is where we test our bikes, but our inspiration comes from riding through Amsterdam, Copenhagen, and beyond. The result? Durability built for city streets, infused with the best of global cycling culture.

URL: https://brooklynbicycleco.com/collections/bike-components-brooklyn-bicycle-co
================================================================================
I've had my Roebling for around six months now. It's been a good experience. The key use for me is for utility rides, but sometimes for athletic rides too. Everything has really checked out with it. Brakes, gears, tires, etc. The layout with the bike tends to support my efficient ride style, where I want the speed I can get from it, where I'm tucked into it, and am able to respond to any traffic problems. I did have one flat early on with it and patched it. For myself, while the tires are good, and are the same size compared to what I sized down to on my Marin hybrid (35C) I'm planning on sizing down to 32C with this for the speed and city streets. I did have an issue with the left rear brake arm locking on me, but when I removed the arm, it was obviously related to too much crud built up or left. Cleaned and lubed and it straightened out for me. The other three haven't done that yet, but now I know the take down for those. The 29 pound weight is reasonable and three lbs. under what the Marin weighs, and five over what my steel State road bike weighs.

================================================================================
Get Your Bike in 3 Easy Steps
1. Choose your dream bike, and order online
Choose your perfect ride from one of our signature collections. Once you have your bike picked out, simply check out like you would any online purchase. Have a question along the way? We're always here to help! Check out our: Bike Finder Quiz, Bike Buying Guide, or simply contact us with any questions.
2. We'll ship your bike to your local bike shop for assembly
Once your order is placed, our team will hand-select the assembly shop based off your provided shipping address and other factors. (Please enter your correct shipping address during checkout!) Your bike will then ship directly to the shop we select for professional assembly. We'll keep you updated every step of the way via email.
3. You pick up your bike from the shop and roll out the door!
We will notify you when your bike is ready to ride. We ask for 12-14 business days from the time of shipment to the time you'll be riding. Once your bike is ready, all you'll have to do is head in to the assembly shop, pick it up, and roll out the door. It's that simple!
For USA customers, another option is to order your bike through our website and have it shipped to their shop for professional assembly, as all of our US Stocking Dealers are also Assembly Partners. Please note: If you want to test ride one of our bikes, we strongly encourage calling your local shop prior to visiting to confirm they have the size/style you're interested in.
Please note: If there's no Assembly Partner near you, don’t worry. We’ll work with a local bike shop to get you taken care of. If you have a special request on where you would like your bicycle built, please reach out to us directly and we will do our best to accommodate your request.

URL: https://brooklynbicycleco.com/pages/warranty
================================================================================
Warranty & Returns
If you're not 100% satisfied with your Brooklyn Bicycle, please reach out to us within 30 days, and we'll do everything we can to resolve the issue. One of the reasons we partner with bike shops around the country via our Buy & Ride program is to ensure that your bike is in perfect working order, from the hands of a professional mechanic, before it ever reaches you.
Warranty
Frame
We’re so confident in the quality of our products that our frames are warrantied against manufacturing defects for life.
- Warranty is only valid for the original owner and is non-transferable. Proof of purchase must be provided.
- Manufacturing defects do not include damage resulting from improper use, lack of maintenance, user-incurred damage, accidents, and/or wear and tear, including gearing and brake adjustments.
Parts
Brooklyn Bicycle Co. offers a one-year warranty against manufacturing defects on the following:
- Brakes, Calipers, and Rotors
- Drivetrains
- Handlebars/Stems
- Pedals
- Seat Posts
- Wheels and Hubs
The following are not warrantied:
- Brake Pads
- Chains
- Cosmetic Blemishes
- Fenders
- Spokes
- Grips
- Paint
- Saddles
- Tire Treads and Tubes
- Tire Blooming
Activating Your Warranty
We recommend registering your warranty. Please note your warranty is only activated upon professional assembly of your bike, and proof of professional assembly must be provided for any warranty claim.
Additionally, please note we do not capture serial numbers; we will only have your serial number on file if you register it with us. Your serial number can be found stamped into your frame's bottom bracket underneath where the pedal arms pass through.
How to Submit a Warranty Inquiry
You can submit your warranty inquiry through our Warranty Inquiry Submission Form:
- A photo or video of the issue
- A description of the issue
- Your serial number
Please note a photo and serial number are essential to processing your submission.
Return & Exchange Policy
The most common reason for returns is an issue with sizing. Please view our products’ size guides to ensure you’re ordering the correct size bicycle and reach out to us if you have any doubts regarding what size bike you should buy. To help you avoid our restocking fee, please reach out to us via phone at 800.631.0630, schedule a one-on-one consultation call with our Concierge line, live chat on our website, or contact us if you have sizing concerns.
For anything other than sizing, we can usually resolve most issues without resorting to a return, so please contact us. If you'd still like to return, please note that returns must be initiated within 30 days of your purchase date (or within 30 days of shipment, for back-ordered items).
CANCELLATIONS: If you need to cancel your order and your order or pre-order has not shipped, please reach out immediately. Our team processes orders quickly, but we will make every effort to catch it before it goes out the door. Please note since every order is treated as a firm order and inventory is being allocated, cancellations will incur a 5% cancellation fee. That's absolutely not made to be punitive, it just covers the cost of processing fees that we have incurred on your transaction. If you are uncertain about the bike or size that may be right for you, please reach out to us prior to placing your order or pre-order.
Return & Exchange Policy for Bicycles
Returned and exchanged bicycles that were purchased directly from Brooklyn Bicycle Co. incur a flat $200 restocking fee. We understand that a restocking fee of $200.00 seems excessive, however we pay to have the original bike shipped and assembled by your local bike shop, then disassembled and shipped back us. All of this is included in the flat restocking fee.
All purchases on discounted bicycles are final sale, including all promotional discounts, sales, and coupons. All factory deal items are final sale.
Return & Exchange Policy for Accessories
Brooklyn Bicycle Co. will accept returns of new, unopened, and unused and/or defective merchandise by mail. Please note that returns must be initiated within 30 days of your purchase (or within 30 days of shipment for backordered items only).
Shipping fees—both outbound and return—are non-refundable. If the item you are returning originally shipped for free, we will refund your payment less the current cost of shipping for similar items.
Please contact us regarding your return. We will provide you with a Returns Authorization Number, which you are required to write on the outside of your package to expedite your return process.
Any items included in a sale, or purchased with a promotional code or coupon, are final sale. All factory deal items are final sale. Please note any free promotional items you may have received separately must also be returned in new condition with your return, otherwise the full retail value will be deducted from your refund amount.
Due to hygienic reasons, please note helmets are final sale, and are ineligible for returns or exchanges.

Love the bike. Live in a hilly terrain and it is perfect. Have had it for about a month and have been riding it 50+ miles everyday; no complaints.
Four decades after I last rode a bike I decided to get back in the saddle, to help with the balance and strength issues associated with getting, ummmm, more mature. After lots of research, I chose the Franklin 3-speed. It not only came with all of the features I was looking for, but it's the most beautifully designed bike on the market. The 29 pound weight is reasonable and three lbs. under what the Marin weighs, and five over what my steel State road bike weighs.

What makes the perfect beach cruiser bike?
Upright Riding Position
Because cruiser bikes are as much about seeing as they are about being seen.
Cushy Sprung Saddle
For keeping your bottom comfortable for as far as the road will take you.
All-Weather Components
For ensuring your bikes looks and rides amazing for as long as it's at your side.
Professional Assembly
Made for easy cruising—your Brooklyn cruiser bike will be professionally assembled at a local shop for a relaxed, worry-free ride—at no cost to you.
Expert Opinions on Our Cruiser Bikes
Have Cruiser Bike Questions?
We Have Answers.
Get quick solutions to commonly asked questions about cruiser bikes.
What is a cruiser bike?
Cruiser bikes are comfortable, stylish bicycles with wide tires, a relaxed riding position, and a simple design, perfect for casual rides.
What is a beach cruiser bike?
Beach cruiser bikes are a type of cruiser bike designed for relaxed coastal rides, featuring wide cruiser tires, an upright position, a comfortable saddle, and a durable frame suited for sandy paths.
What size cruiser bike do I need?
Like any bike, sizing is NOT universal across bike brands or bike. Just because you ride a medium in one brand doesn’t mean every medium-sized bike will fit the same. Geometry varies across brands and even between models, and these design differences affect sizing. Always check the size chart for the specific bike you’re considering.
What is a cruiser bike good for?
A good cruiser bike delivers an abundance of comfort and easy riding fun. A cruiser bike is great for leisurely rides, beach paths, and relaxed city cruising, offering a smooth, easygoing ride with a comfortable upright position.
Why are Brooklyn's cruiser bikes better?
It boils down to three things:
- We believe professional assembly is not a convenience, it's a necessity, that's why we include professional assembly on every bike purchase- free of charge to you.
- Our reviews speak for themselves. We stand behind our products, use premium components, and offer a lifetime warranty on frames and forks.
- Brooklyn is where we test our cruiser bikes, but our cruiser bike inspiration comes from riding through Amsterdam, Copenhagen, and beyond. The result? Durability built for city streets, infused with the best of global cycling culture.

Great question, and one we are asked often. The Willow 7 is a beast of its own and features an internally geared 7-speed rear hub, hydraulic disc brakes, an aluminum frame, and internal cable routing. Additionally, you may have noticed that the Franklin 3 and the Franklin 8 are the same price-- this isn't a mistake! The Franklin 3 features a 3-speed internally geared rear hub (hello, super low-maintenance and ultra-durability), while the Franklin 8 features a 1x8 drivetrain with an external derailleur (hello, wider gearing range). There's a trade-off between the two, but the better choice will truly depend upon your use case. You can read more about the similarities and differences here.
Absolutely! Step-through models are well suited for carrying cargo because they're so easy to mount without having to throw your leg over a top tube. This makes mounting a rear child seat a breeze. I've had my Roebling for around six months now. It's been a good experience. The key use for me is for utility rides, but sometimes for athletic rides too. Everything has really checked out with it. Great question, and one we are asked often. You may have noticed that the Franklin 3 and the Franklin 8 are the same price-- this isn't a mistake! The Franklin 3 features a 3-speed internally geared rear hub (hello, super low-maintenance and ultra-durability), while the Franklin 8 features a 1x8 drivetrain with an external derailleur (hello, wider gearing range). There's a trade-off between the two, but the better choice will truly depend upon your use case. What makes the perfect step through bike?
Step Through Geometry
For easy riding with no wardrobe restrictions and a light, yet absorbent ride
Upright Riding Position
For easy and comfortable riding, with reduced pressure on your wrists and back
Premium Componentry
For a bike equal parts style and durability, ensuring your step-through looks and rides amazingly
Professional Assembly
Easy to ride, effortless to own—your Brooklyn step through bike will be professionally assembled at a local shop for a smooth, comfortable ride—at no cost to you.
Step Through Bikes
Expert Opinions on Our Step Through Bikes
Have Step Through Bike Questions?
We Have Answers.
Get quick solutions to commonly asked questions about step through bikes.
What is a step through bike?
A step-through bike has a low or no top tube, making it easy to mount and dismount. It’s great for comfort, casual riding, riding with no wardrobe restrictions, riding with tall cargo or children, and those with mobility concerns.
Can I put a child seat on a step through bike?
Absolutely! Step-through models are well suited for carrying cargo because they're so easy to mount without having to throw your leg over a top tube. This makes mounting a rear child seat a breeze. NOTE: We always recommend using the rear carrier from the child seat manufacturer as it will have been tested for load capacity.
Why are Brooklyn's step through bikes better?
It boils down to three things:
- We believe professional assembly is not a convenience, it's a necessity, that's why we include professional assembly on every bike purchase- free of charge to you.
- Our reviews speak for themselves. We stand behind our products, use premium components, and offer a lifetime warranty on frames and forks.
- Brooklyn is where we test our bikes, but our inspiration comes from riding through Amsterdam, Copenhagen, and beyond. The result? Durability built for city streets, infused with the best of global cycling culture.

Great question, and one we are asked often. You may have noticed that the Franklin 3 and the Franklin 8 are the same price-- this isn't a mistake! The Franklin 3 features a 3-speed internally geared rear hub (hello, super low-maintenance and ultra-durability), while the Franklin 8 features a 1x8 drivetrain with an external derailleur (hello, wider gearing range). There's a trade-off between the two, but the better choice will truly depend upon your use case. URL: https://brooklynbicycleco.com/collections/locks
================================================================================
I've had my Roebling for around six months now. It's been a good experience. The key use for me is for utility rides, but sometimes for athletic rides too. Everything has really checked out with it. Brakes, gears, tires, etc. The layout with the bike tends to support my efficient ride style, where I want the speed I can get from it, where I'm tucked into it, and am able to respond to any traffic problems. I did have one flat early on with it and patched it. For myself, while the tires are good, and are the same size compared to what I sized down to on my Marin hybrid (35C) I'm planning on sizing down to 32C with this for the speed and city streets. I did have an issue with the left rear brake arm locking on me, but when I removed the arm, it was obviously related to too much crud built up or left. Cleaned and lubed and it straightened out for me. The other three haven't done that yet, but now I know the take down for those. The 29 pound weight is reasonable and three lbs. under what the Marin weighs, and five over what my steel State road bike weighs.

But don’t let this sleek hybrid bike fool you: It’s just as ideal for commuting and running errands as it is for working up a sweat on city streets or bike paths. While similar in design to its sibling the Roebling, the Lorimer sets itself apart with a more compact cockpit and wider saddle.

I've had my Roebling for around six months now. It's been a good experience. The key use for me is for utility rides, but sometimes for athletic rides too. Everything has really checked out with it. Brakes, gears, tires, etc. The layout with the bike tends to support my efficient ride style, where I want the speed I can get from it, where I'm tucked into it, and am able to respond to any traffic problems. I did have one flat early on with it and patched it. For myself, while the tires are good, and are the same size compared to what I sized down to on my Marin hybrid (35C) I'm planning on sizing down to 32C with this for the speed and city streets. I did have an issue with the left rear brake arm locking on me, but when I removed the arm, it was obviously related to too much crud built up or left. Cleaned and lubed and it straightened out for me. The other three haven't done that yet, but now I know the take down for those. The 29 pound weight is reasonable and three lbs. under what the Marin weighs, and five over what my steel State road bike weighs.

Expert Opinion on Our Hybrid Bikes
Have Hybrid Bike Questions?
We Have Answers.
Get quick solutions to commonly asked questions about hybrid bikes.
What is a hybrid bike?
The word "hybrid" has become a much overused term. It's the product of brands trying to market their bikes as the best and most versatile. In reality, hybrid bikes have become a category of their own, borrowing from many styles of bike and merging them to create a great bike. Hybrid bikes often boast a flat bar, a wide gearing range, a lightweight frame, and are suitable for a wide range of use cases, from trail/gravel riding to city commuting to fitness riding.
What size hybrid bike do I need?
Like any bike, sizing is NOT universal across bike brands or bike. Just because you ride a medium in one brand doesn’t mean every medium-sized bike will fit the same. Geometry varies across brands and even between models, and these design differences affect sizing. Always check the size chart for the specific bike you’re considering.
What's the difference between the Lorimer hybrid and Roebling hybrid bike?
While similar in design to its sibling the Roebling, the Lorimer sets itself apart with a more compact cockpit and wider saddle. It's ideal for our more petite riders from 4'10" - 5'7". The Roebling comes in an extended range of sizes, covering riders from 4'11"-6'4".
What is chromoly steel?
Chromoly (short for chromium-molybdenum) is a premium, lighter grade of steel prized by cyclists. It retains steel's legendary durability and shock absorption while shedding unnecessary weight—giving you a smoother, lighter, and more responsive ride. Specifically, our Roebling and Lorimer's chromoly is double-butted which means the tubing is thicker at the stress points (welds) and then tapers at the center of the tubes resulting in an even lighter bike.
Why are Brooklyn's hybrid bikes better?
It boils down to three things:
- We believe professional assembly is not a convenience, it's a necessity, that's why we include professional assembly on every bike purchase- free of charge to you.
- Our reviews speak for themselves. We stand behind our products, use premium components, and offer a lifetime warranty on frames and forks.
- Brooklyn is our playground and our testing ground. Why does that matter? Our bikes endure some of the roughest, pothole-filled streets out there—proving their durability and longevity where it counts.

Get quick solutions to commonly asked commuter bike questions.
What is a commuter bike?
Simply put, a commuter bike is a practical, comfortable bicycle designed for daily travel, featuring a durable frame, upright position, and the capability to add accessories for urban riding.
What makes a good commuter bike?
A good commuter bike is lightweight, durable, and comfortable, with reliable brakes, wide gearing, puncture-resistant tires, and mounts for accessorizing.
What to look for in a commuter bike?
Look for comfort, durability, and versatility—features like mounts for racks or fenders, puncture-resistant tires, and a frame suited for your commute's distance and terrain.

URL: https://brooklynbicycleco.com/collections/bike-baskets
================================================================================
I've had my Roebling for around six months now. It's been a good experience. The key use for me is for utility rides, but sometimes for athletic rides too. Everything has really checked out with it. Brakes, gears, tires, etc. The layout with the bike tends to support my efficient ride style, where I want the speed I can get from it, where I'm tucked into it, and am able to respond to any traffic problems. I did have one flat early on with it and patched it. For myself, while the tires are good, and are the same size compared to what I sized down to on my Marin hybrid (35C) I'm planning on sizing down to 32C with this for the speed and city streets. I did have an issue with the left rear brake arm locking on me, but when I removed the arm, it was obviously related to too much crud built up or left. Cleaned and lubed and it straightened out for me. The other three haven't done that yet, but now I know the take down for those. The 29 pound weight is reasonable and three lbs. under what the Marin weighs, and five over what my steel State road bike weighs.

URL: https://brooklynbicycleco.com/pages/our-factory
================================================================================
Experience our Factories
Our supplier partnerships span multiple decades, hundreds of trips around the world and countless in-home meals celebrating our friendship. While our final assembly takes place in Taiwan - the bicycle capital of the world - we source our parts from many countries.
Our manufacturing facilities are a source of incredible pride.
We’ve worked hard to find factories that treat their employees as fairly as we do, treat our products with the care that we do, and care about the safety and well-being of our customers as much as we do.
We know, because we’re there. We regularly visit our factories (more than our significant others care for). We have overseas consultants on site for every production run. And we only ever sign a contract with a factory after putting them through a strict vetting process. We work exclusively with factories that meet or exceed our stringent standards, and we walk away from any factory that we wouldn’t be comfortable showcasing to the world.
Among other rubrics, we’re guided by several core beliefs which help us determine whether a given factory is a good fit:
* Our manufacturing partners' employees should be treated similarly to how we treat our own
* Everyone deserves access to living wage jobs
* Everyone should be treated with respect and dignity in the workplace
* Worker safety and quality control matter every day
* The quality of every single component on our bikes matters
* Quality products are sustainable products
We would happily invite any of our customers to spend time on the floor of our factories and converse with our factory employees. However, in case you don’t feel like booking a ticket to the other side of the world, we invite you to check out the video above for a sneak peek into how Brooklyn bikes are made.

Great question, and one we are asked often. You may have noticed that the Franklin 3 and the Franklin 8 are the same price-- this isn't a mistake! The Franklin 3 features a 3-speed internally geared rear hub (hello, super low-maintenance and ultra-durability), while the Franklin 8 features a 1x8 drivetrain with an external derailleur (hello, wider gearing range). There's a trade-off between the two, but the better choice will truly depend upon your use case. Absolutely! Step-through models are well suited for carrying cargo because they're so easy to mount without having to throw your leg over a top tube. This makes mounting a rear child seat a breeze. On my maiden voyage around the neighborhood, people yelled out "Nice bike!" I've never gotten so much attention!
The Franklin is a dream to ride; it's lightweight, the gears shift easily, and the handlebars are the perfect width and angle. I ordered the rear rack separately and installed it myself with ease.
My biggest concern was the logistics of having to pick up the bike at a local shop, but that process was flawless. URL: https://brooklynbicycleco.com/pages/bike-buying-guide-brooklyn-bicycle-co
================================================================================
I've had my Roebling for around six months now. It's been a good experience. The key use for me is for utility rides, but sometimes for athletic rides too. Everything has really checked out with it. Brakes, gears, tires, etc. The layout with the bike tends to support my efficient ride style, where I want the speed I can get from it, where I'm tucked into it, and am able to respond to any traffic problems. I did have one flat early on with it and patched it. For myself, while the tires are good, and are the same size compared to what I sized down to on my Marin hybrid (35C) I'm planning on sizing down to 32C with this for the speed and city streets. I did have an issue with the left rear brake arm locking on me, but when I removed the arm, it was obviously related to too much crud built up or left. Cleaned and lubed and it straightened out for me. The other three haven't done that yet, but now I know the take down for those. The 29 pound weight is reasonable and three lbs. under what the Marin weighs, and five over what my steel State road bike weighs.

Stylish, lightweight, and most importantly, fun to ride.
We only do one fixie bike for a reason: we've done it right, without cutting corners. Commuter bike by day, track bike by night: Our lightweight fixie bike stands apart from other fixed gear bikes thanks to its lightweight chromoly frame and easy rolling sealed-bearing hubs. With its flip-flop hub giving you easy access to either drivetrain, the Wythe’s puncture-resistant tires ensure a low maintenance, worry-free ride. This bike is packed with everything you need and nothing you don’t, so you can ride your way all day.
- Premium single speed drivetrain
- Ultra-lightweight chromoly frame

- Flip-flop hub

Go on paved bicycle trails only and have ridden well over 1,500 miles this summer and fall. Both bikes ride smoothly and get compliments on their retro looks. Love the fenders!
I’ve ridden my new bike almost every day since I received it the beginning of July, and it’s been an absolute pleasure. I use it for my daily commute (only 4 miles one way) as well as most of my errands, and I use the Topeak rear rack to carry everything. It’s a very comfortable bike, and the 8-speed range is perfect for my area where it’s relatively flat with a few small hills here and there. It’s a well built bicycle, with quality components that are simple and robust.

Not the lightest bike, but for an 8 speed sleek bike, not bad in weight-I’m still able to carry it up and down short flights of stairs without hassle. Assembly and delivery to my door were so easy and a huge reason why I purchased this bike. Only hassle was my service delivered without assessing my height. But was easy to find a bike shop to adjust for me. Other bike companies tack on a large fee for the assembly for an already expensive bike, and even then they required pick up. Overall very happy with price point for the comfort, ride quality, aesthetic, and easy delivery experience.
The Virginia countryside is not normally the playground of Brooklyn bicycles. But my Franklin does well here after a few minor modifications and add-ons. With a quill stem (silver, of course) allowing for a lower handlebar, this step-through moves at a good clip, even when there's a headwind. It's also the kind of bike you can't just park and walk away from. After a few steps, you have to turn around and give it an admiring look. It's that stylish. Great job, Brooklyn!

I would highly recommend this bike and this company.

I've had my Roebling for around six months now. It's been a good experience. The key use for me is for utility rides, but sometimes for athletic rides too. Everything has really checked out with it. Brakes, gears, tires, etc. The layout with the bike tends to support my efficient ride style, where I want the speed I can get from it, where I'm tucked into it, and am able to respond to any traffic problems. I did have one flat early on with it and patched it. For myself, while the tires are good, and are the same size compared to what I sized down to on my Marin hybrid (35C) I'm planning on sizing down to 32C with this for the speed and city streets. I did have an issue with the left rear brake arm locking on me, but when I removed the arm, it was obviously related to too much crud built up or left. Cleaned and lubed and it straightened out for me. The other three haven't done that yet, but now I know the take down for those. The 29 pound weight is reasonable and three lbs. under what the Marin weighs, and five over what my steel State road bike weighs.

Comfortable, Reliable, and Stylish
Our essential city bike is a fan favorite for getting around town without working up a sweat. Stylish and utilitarian, the Franklin 8 enables riders of all stripes to simply hop on and go. The step-through frame and color-matched fenders ensure easy riding sans wardrobe restrictions, while the lightweight design makes for smooth travel from brunch, to the office, and everywhere in between. With a wide range 8-speed drivetrain and puncture-resistant tires allowing you to conquer varied terrain, this bike is as versatile as it is stylish.
- External 8-speed. Also available in a 3-speed internal
- Relaxed and upright geometry
- Mounting points for carriers
FRAME
Frame: Lightweight Steel frame & rear carrier braze-ons
Fork: Brooklyn Bicycle Co. custom steel; front carrier braze-ons
DRIVETRAIN
Front Hub: Sealed-Bearing Alloy 32H
Rear Hub: Sealed-Bearing Alloy 32H
Shifter: 8-Speed Twist-Shifter
Derailleur: Shimano 8-Speed
Pedals: Brooklyn Bicycle Co. Alloy City Pedals
Bottom Bracket: Square-taper BSA English threaded-cartridge bottom bracket with sealed bearings
Crank: 3-piece alloy with replaceable chain ring & integrated chain guard
Chain: KMC Heavy Duty
WHEELS
Tires: M - Puncture-resistant 26 x 1 3/8” tires; L - Puncture-resistant 700c x 35 tires
Rims: Double-Walled Alloy, 32H with CNC Machined Brake Surface
Spokes: 14G stainless steel
COMPONENTS
Saddle: Selle Royal Vegan Leather Comfort Saddle
Seat Post: Alloy with integrated seat clamp and micro-adjust
Handlebar: Brooklyn Bicycle Co. Custom relaxed ride alloy handlebars & 180mm alloy stem
Headset: 1” Threaded Alloy, Semi-sealed with weather-seal ring
Grips: Brooklyn Bicycle Co. Custom Vegan Leather Comfort Grips
Front & Rear Brakes: Tektro Single-Pivot Caliper with Tektro Alloy Hand Levers
OTHER INFORMATION
Extras: Heavy-duty alloy center-mount kickstand
Extras: Color-matched fenders and chainguard
Weight: 31-33 lbs

Every bicycle includes free shipping AND professional assembly through our Buy & Ride Program. Once your bike ships, you'll be riding within 14 business days.
Our number one priority is making your bicycle purchase as simple as possible- while also ensuring the bicycle you receive is up to our stringent quality requirements. That's why we ship every one of our bicycles to a hand-selected local bike shop for professional assembly. Once it's fully assembled and ready to ride, you simply head into the shop and roll out the door!
Our industry leading Buy & Ride program was the first of its kind. Through years of experience, and a large network of partner shops across the country, we've perfected the process.
There are several factors to consider when choosing your new bike, and we're here to help!
We highly recommend reading our Bike Buying Guide, along with watching the video. You'll want to consider your preferences on things like frame style and gearing range, and take into account how you'll be using your bike, and on what terrain.
We also recommend taking our Bike Finder Quiz which will point you in the right direction based upon your considerations.
And finally, it's important to note that bike sizing is not universal across brands, nor across models. Each bike has a unique geometry which means different sizing. Please consult the sizing guide recommendations.
If you have any questions or are between sizes, please contact us for personalized guidance and we'd happy to assist!
Brooklyn Bicycle Co. frames and forks are warrantied against manufacturing defects for life under the original owner, while parts and hardware are guaranteed for one year after purchase. This warranty is non-transferrable and covers manufacturing defects, not user-incurred damage, unforeseen accidents, and/or everyday wear and tear. You can read our full warranty here.
If you're still unsure of sizing, or just want confirmation that you’re making the right choice, give us a call at 800.631.0630, live chat with a team member, or contact us. You can also schedule a call with our Bike Concierge.
We understand that a bicycle is a big investment, and we want you to be 100% confident your choice.
Great question, and one we are asked often. You may have noticed that the Franklin 3 and the Franklin 8 are the same price-- this isn't a mistake! The Franklin 3 features a 3-speed internally geared rear hub (hello, super low-maintenance and ultra-durability), while the Franklin 8 features a 1x8 drivetrain with an external derailleur (hello, wider gearing range). There's a trade-off between the two, but the better choice will truly depend upon your use case. The Willow 7 is a premium super commuter featuring an aluminum frame, an internal 7-speed hub, and a hydraulic disc brakes. You can read more about the similarities and differences here.
Absolutely! Step-through models are well suited for carrying cargo because they're so easy to mount without having to throw your leg over a top tube. This makes mounting a rear child seat a breeze. NOTE: We always recommend using the rear carrier from the child seat manufacturer as it will have been tested for load capacity.
